HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B002","root_000516/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_020719b8cd7cf64a6a1c","text":"1. **Originating through measured differentiation.** `خَلَقَ` anchors `خ ل ق:B002` (originating and bringing into being) and `خ ل ق:B001` (measuring and proportioning); `ٱلذَّكَر` and `ٱلْأُنثَىٰ` anchor `ذ ك ر:B001` and `ء ن ث:B001`, the opposed sexes. Night/day in 92:1–2 and the sharply separated endeavors in 92:4–8 place this pair inside a repeated structure of differentiated counterparts. The packet supplies pairings; the inferred move is that creation operates by proportioning distinct but related terms. The ayah consequently reads as an account of ordered differentiation, not a bare inventory of two created kinds.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_09b4ab522504f55f6c6b","text":"92:15-17 changed the warning from threat into a protective device: the fire is named so that one may be kept aside from it. The beacon/landmark branch of `ن و ر` gained force because warning, guidance, and avoidance form one rescue mechanism.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000434/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_8393b7b42cdd071f307c","text":"1. **Creation as measured differentiation.** Evidence: `خ ل ق` B001 (measuring/proportioning), B002 (bringing into being), `ذ ك ر` B001 and `ء ن ث` B001 (male/female opposition). Structural cue: this third oath comes before “your striving is diverse.” Mechanism: differentiated creation becomes the structural template for divergent acts. Abductive move: I supply that created difference authorizes the later moral plurality without making the outcomes arbitrary. Reading change: the ayah is not a static biology note; it grounds patterned divergence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000058/B001","root_000434/B001","root_000434/B002","root_000516/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_93779794e035a60c0a52","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:3:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]],[\"92:3:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:3:4\",\"root_000058\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Erkek ile dişinin yaratılması, suredeki karşıt çiftler içinde ölçülü ve ilişkili bir farklılaştırma düzeni kurar; sonraki yolların ayrılığı da bu düzenin ahlaki bir devamı olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000058/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_95d29d141d797a10a918","text":"3. **Womb/fertile land and growth economy.** Evidence: `ء ن ث` B004 (fertile easy land), `ر ح م` B003 from 92:0, `ز ك و` B001 from 92:18. Structural cue: creation is followed by striving and later purification/growth. Mechanism: generative fertility contrasts with the sterile self-sufficiency of 92:8. Abductive move: I infer that created generativity supplies an ecological model for giving: life grows by outgoing transfer. Reading change: 92:3 quietly prepares the later money/growth contrast.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000516/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_b2f620cc40e8062f1ff2","text":"2. **Sharp/soft polarity as latent moral texture.** Evidence: `ذ ك ر` B002 (hard/sharp/forceful), `ء ن ث` B002 (softness/yielding), activated by 92:5 giving/guarding and 92:8 withholding/self-sufficiency. Structural cue: paired qualities precede paired conduct. Mechanism: the roots open a material analogy: force and yielding can become either generosity/protection or hardness/withholding. Abductive move: I infer that sexed terms activate qualities without reducing the later moral agents to sexes. Reading change: the pair becomes a matrix of capacities, not only a male/female listing.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_ba9b49547c40fc2b7826","text":"2. **Generation under mercy’s enclosure.** The basmalah’s repeated `ر ح م` carries the packet’s `ر ح م:B003` womb branch into the window, while `ء ن ث:B001` fixes the female term and `خ ل ق:B002` fixes origination. The structural cue is proximity between womb-capable mercy language and the created sex pair, followed by giving and guarding in 92:5. The abductive move is that enclosure, generation, and protection form one material model; that model is inferred rather than asserted by the syntax. Creation of the pair thereby gains a sheltered, life-bearing dimension alongside sheer origination.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000058/B004","root_000434/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c81d22c7e70cb0c3f070","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:3:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:3:4\",\"root_000058\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Dişiliğin verimli ve kolay toprak boyutu, ilerideki arınma ve büyüme karşısında cimri yeterliliği üretken olmayan bir kapanma olarak okumaya imkân verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_c989bcd227e971b23794","text":"The opposed behavioral chains beginning in 92:5 and 92:8 made the sex pair a structural precedent for differentiation. They also imposed a correction: nothing in the packet maps either conduct onto either sex, so the retained mechanism is measured pairing itself, not a gendered moral assignment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000058/B002","root_000516/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ccb05532ac454f3326b4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:3:3\",\"root_000516\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:3:4\",\"root_000058\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Erillikteki sertlik ile dişilikteki yumuşaklık boyutları, sonraki verme ve esirgeme yollarına cinsiyet atamadan, insan davranışındaki kuvvet ve esneme imkânlarını görünür kılar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000058/B001","root_000434/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f21c743e9298756ebd1c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:3:2\",\"root_000434\",[\"B002\"]],[\"92:3:4\",\"root_000058\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Açılıştaki merhamet ve sonraki sakınma, erkek ile dişinin meydana getirilişine hayatı kuşatan ve koruyan bir üretkenlik boyutu ekler.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
